{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787286394448,"sessionID":"ses_fdd6e26c1ffebKw5N1CWm4q22w","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"skill","callID":"call-15b6f378-26ff-4130-92e0-f98873a59325-0","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"name":"fastapi-python"},"output":"<skill_content name=\"fastapi-python\">\n# Skill: fastapi-python\n\n# FastAPI Python\n\nYou are an expert in FastAPI and Python backend development.\n\n## Key Principles\n\n- Write concise, technical responses with accurate Python examples\n- Favor functional, declarative programming over class-based approaches\n- Prioritize modularization to eliminate code duplication\n- Use descriptive variable names with auxiliary verbs (e.g., `is_active`, `has_permission`)\n- Employ lowercase with underscores for file/directory naming (e.g., `routers/user_routes.py`)\n- Export routes and utilities explicitly\n- Follow the RORO (Receive an Object, Return an Object) pattern\n\n## Python/FastAPI Standards\n\n- Use `def` for pure functions, `async def` for asynchronous operations\n- Use type hints for all function signatures. Prefer Pydantic models over raw dictionaries\n- Structure: exported router, sub-routes, utilities, static content, types (models, schemas)\n- Omit curly braces for single-line conditionals\n- Write concise one-line conditional syntax\n\n## Error Handling\n\n- Handle edge cases at function entry points\n- Employ early returns for error conditions\n- Place happy path logic last\n- Avoid unnecessary else statements; use if-return patterns\n- Implement guard clauses for preconditions\n- Provide proper error logging and user-friendly messaging\n\n## FastAPI-Specific Guidelines\n\n- Use functional components (plain functions) and Pydantic models for input validation\n- Declare routes with clear return type annotations\n- Prefer lifespan context managers for managing startup and shutdown events\n- Leverage middleware for logging, error monitoring, and optimization\n- Use HTTPException for expected errors and model them as specific HTTP responses\n- Apply Pydantic's BaseModel consistently for validation\n\n## Performance Optimization\n\n- Minimize blocking I/O; use async for all database and API calls\n- Implement caching with Redis or in-memory stores\n- Optimize Pydantic serialization/deserialization\n- Use lazy loading for large datasets\n\n## Key Conventions\n\n1. Rely on FastAPI's dependency injection system\n2. Prioritize API performance metrics (response time, latency, throughput)\n3. Structure routes and dependencies for readability and maintainability\n\n## Dependencies\n\nFastAPI, Pydantic v2, asyncpg/aiomysql, SQLAlchemy 2.0\n\nBase directory for this skill: /workspaces/powerchoice/.agents/skills/fastapi-python\nRelative paths in this skill (e.g., scripts/, reference/) are relative to this base directory.\nNote: file list is sampled.\n\n<skill_files>\n\n</skill_files>\n</skill_content>","metadata":{"name":"fastapi-python","dir":"/workspaces/powerchoice/.agents/skills/fastapi-python","truncated":false},"title":"Loaded skill: fastapi-python","time":{"start":1787286394295,"end":1787286394429}},"id":"prt_022921471001R7y94VCJBA3tmq","sessionID":"ses_fdd6e26c1ffebKw5N1CWm4q22w","messageID":"msg_02291ed7d001q2CPev4ejSLxfK"}}

 enqueued = AiAgentJob.enqueue_baseline(\n873:             session=session,\n874:             customer_id=int(project.customer_id),\n875:             project_id=int(project.id),\n876: no_providers_message,\n        resolve_llm_credentials,\n        unique_voter_models,\n    )\n    from ..utils.ai.identity import build_ai_participant_key\n\n    _require_ai_admin(usr_context)\n    project = _require_project(session, usr_context, form.project_id)\n    customer = usr_context.sess_customer\n    if resolve_llm_credentials(customer) is None:\n        return AiBaselineJobResult(failure_reason=no_providers_message())\n    alts, factors = _load_content(session, project)\n    active_alts = [a for a in alts if not getattr(a, \"disabled\", False)]\n    if len(active_alts) < 2:\n        return AiBaselineJobResult(failure_reason=\"Save at least two options before running AI agents\")\n    requested = unique_voter_models(form.models)\n    if not requested:\n        requested = unique_voter_models(getattr(project, \"ai_voter_models\", None))\n    if not requested:\n        requested = [DEFAULT_MODEL_KEY]\n    existing_parts = ProjectVoteParticipant.list_for_project_system(\n        session=session, customer_id=project.customer_id, project_id=project.id, clear_lock=False\n    )\n    parts_by_key = _ai_parts_by_key(existing_parts)\n    views: list[AiBaselineJobView] = []\n    latest = None\n    all_complete = True\n    for model_key in requested:\n        creds = resolve_llm_credentials(customer, model_key=model_key)\n        if creds is None:\n            return AiBaselineJobResult(failure_reason=no_providers_message())\n        part = parts_by_key.get(build_ai_participant_key(creds.model_key))\n        if part is not None and part.is_complete:\n            views.append(_job_view(None, participant_complete=True, display_name=creds.display_name))\n            continue\n        all_complete = False\n        enqueued = AiAgentJob.enqueue_baseline(\n            session=session,\n            customer_id=int(project.customer_id),\n            project_id=int(project.id),\n            requested_by_user_id=int(usr_context.sess_user.id),\n            model_key=creds.model_key,\n            details={\"provider\": creds.provider, \"source\": creds.source, \"display_name\": creds.display_name},\n            clear_lock=False,\n        )\n        latest = enqueued.job_info\n        views.append(_job_view(enqueued.job_info, participant_complete=False, display_name=creds.display_name))\n    return AiBaselineJobResult(\n        job=_job_view(latest, \"/project-votes/ai-baseline-status\",\n    summary=\"Return AI agent job status for a project; customer_admin required\",\n    tags=[\"project-votes\"],\n)\nasync def ai_baseline_status(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n    project_id: int,\n) -> AiBaselineJobResult:\n    from ..db.models.ai_agent_jobs import AiAgentJob\n    from ..utils.ai.identity import ai_display_name\n\n    _require_ai_admin(usr_context)\n    project = _require_project(session, usr_context, project_id)\n    jobs = AiAgentJob.list_for_project(\n        session=session, customer_id=project.customer_id, project_id=project.id, clear_lock=False\n    )\n    parts = ProjectVoteParticipant.list_for_project_system(\n        session=session, customer_id=project.customer_id, project_id=project.id, clear_lock=True\n    )\n    parts_by_key = _ai_parts_by_key(parts)\n    views = []\n    complete_count = 0\n    for job in jobs:\n        part = parts_by_key.get(ProjectVoteParticipant.build_ai_key(job.model_key))\n        done = bool(part and part.is_complete)\n        if done:\n            complete_count += 1\n        views.append(\n            _job_view(\n                job,\n                participant_complete=done,\n                display_name=getattr(part, \"display_name\", None) or ai_display_name(job.model_key),\n            )\n        )\n    ai_parts = [p for p in parts if is_ai_participant(p)]\n    all_complete = bool(ai_parts) and all(p.is_complete for p in ai_parts) and not any(\n        j.status in (\"queued\", \"in_progress\") for j in jobs\n    )\n    latest = jobs[0] if jobs else None\n    return AiBaselineJobResult(\n        job=_job_view(latest, participant_complete=all_complete) if latest else None,\n        jobs=views,\n        already_complete=all_complete,\n    )","lineStart":800,"lineEnd":935,"totalLines":935,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/api/app_project_vote_events.py","time":{"start":1787286405003,"end":1787286405382}},"id":"prt_022923dd80011iO6ko8XRJ0gkg","sessionID":"ses_fdd6e26c1ffebKw5N1CWm4q22w","messageID":"msg_0229223ef0016PhHpA6qvAJ1g5"}}

settings._inject_startup_values({\"ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS\": False})\n32:         pvf_settings._inject_startup_values({\"ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS\": False})\n33:         monkeypatch.setattr(settings, \"AI_API_KEY\", \"\")\n34:         monkeypatch.setattr(pvf_settings, \"AI_API_KEY\", \"\")\n35: \n36: \n37: def test_context_ai_flag_default_false_and_secrets_absent(client, account):\n38: account.admin.id)\n54:     with _ai_enabled(monkeypatch):\n55:         response = client.post(\"/ws/core/get-user-customer-context\", json={\"include_account_status\": False})\n56:     assert test_build_report_omits_incomplete_ai_baseline():\n    alts = [{\"id\": 1, \"alternative_title\": \"A\"}, {\"id\": 2, \"alternative_title\": \"B\"}]\n    ai = {\n        \"id\": 11,\n        \"participant_id\": 11,\n        \"source\": \"ai\",\n        \"participant_key\": AI_PARTICIPANT_KEY,\n        \"is_complete\": False,\n        \"display_name\": AI_DISPLAY_NAME,\n    }\n    ai_group _ = (a1, a2)\n\n\ndef test_partition_vote_data_splits_ai():\n    humans, hgroups, ais, agroups = partition_vote_data(\n        [\n            {\"id\": 1, \"source\": \"session\"},\n must be customer_admin in same customer\",\n             tags=['manage-projects'])\nasync def update_customer_project(session: SessionDep, usr_context: UserAccessDep, update_project: CustomerProjectForm) -> CustomerProjectResult_One_Id:\n    check_result = CustomerProject.get_customer_project_by_tag(\n        session=session, tag=update_project.project_tag, usr_context=usr_context, clear_lock=False\n    )\n\n    if check_result.failure_reason or check_result.customer_project_info is None:\n        return CustomerProjectResult_One_Id(failure_reason=check_result.failure_reason, log_id=check_result.log_id, customer_project_id=-1)\n\n    customer_project_info = check_result.customer_project_info\n\n    was_private = bool(customer_project_info.private_participation)\n    wants_private = bool(update_project.private_participation)\n    if was_private and not wants_private:\n        is_sysadmin = int(getattr(usr_context.sess_user, \"system_user_mode\", 0) or 0) >= 2\n        if not is_sysadmin and private_participation_is_locked(\n            session, project=customer_project_info, clear_lock=False\n        ):\n            log_id = log_event(\n                \"private_participation_locked\",\n                usr_context=usr_context,\n                severity=2,\n                details_str=f\"project_tag={customer_project_info.project_tag}\",\n            )","lineStart":580,"lineEnd":629,"totalLines":747,"truncated":true}},"title":"src/api/app_customer_project_management.py","time":{"start":1787286467196,"end":1787286469244}},"id":"prt_022933145001FFVcHIukW44IQR","sessionID":"ses_fdd6e26c1ffebKw5N1CWm4q22w","messageID":"msg_02292d65600101puQ6bjMzSyAn"}}

test_ai_baseline_run_and_mocked_worker(client, account, monkeypatch):\n    project = create_project(account, title=\"Office move\")\n    a1 = create_alternative(account, project_id=project.id, title=\"Downtown\")\n    a2 = create_alternative(account, project_id=project.id, title=\"Suburb\")\n    create_factor(account, project_id=project.id, title=\"Cost\")\n    as_user(client, account.admin.id)\n\n    monkeypatch.setattr(\"src.utils.ai.worker.chat_text_sync\", lambda **_k: \"LEFT\")\n    with _ai_enabled(monkeypatch):\n        res = client.post(\"/ws/project-votes/ai-baseline-run\", json={\"project_id\": project.id})","newString":"def test_ai_baseline_run_skipped_when_include_off(client, account, monkeypatch):\n    project = create_project(account, title=\"Office move\")\n    create_alternative(account, project_id=project.id, title=\"Downtown\")\n    create_alternative(account, project_id=project.id, title=\"Suburb\")\n    as_user(client, account.admin.id)\n    with _ai_enabled(monkeypatch):\n        _save_ai_voters(client, project, include=False, models=[\"glm-5.2\", \"grok-4.6\"])\n        res = client.post(\n            \"/ws/project-votes/ai-baseline-run\",\n            json={\"project_id\": project.id, \"models\": [\"glm-5.2\"]},\n        )\n        assert res.status_code == 200, res.text\n        body = res.json()\n        assert body.get(\"failure_reason\")\n        assert not (body.get(\"jobs\") or [])\n        assert _ai_jobs(project) == []\n        assert _ai_parts(project) == []\n\n\ndef test_ai_baseline_run_empty_selection_when_include_on(client, account, monkeypatch):\n    project = create_project(account, title=\"Office move\")\n    create_alternative(account, project_id=project.id, title=\"Downtown\")\n    create_alternative(account, project_id=project.id, title=\"Suburb\")\n    as_user(client, account.admin.id)\n    with _ai_enabled(monkeypatch):\n        _save_ai_voters(client, project, include=True, models=[])\n        res = client.post(\"/ws/project-votes/ai-baseline-run\", json={\"project_id\": project.id})\n        assert res.status_code == 200, res.text\n        body = res.json()\n        assert body.get(\"failure_reason\")\n        assert not (body.get(\"jobs\") or [])\n        assert _ai_jobs(project) == []\n\n\ndef test_ai_baseline_run_uses_saved_models_not_request(client, account, monkeypatch):\n    project = create_project(account, title=\"Office move\")\n    create_alternative(account, project_id=project.id, title=\"Downtown\")\n    create_alternative(account, project_id=project.id, title=\"Suburb\")\n    as_user(client, account.admin.id)\n    with _ai_enabled(monkeypatch):\n        _save_ai_voters(client, project, include=True, models=[\"glm-5.2\"])\n        res = client.post(\n            \"/ws/project-votes/ai-baseline-run\",\n            json={\"project_id\": project.id, \"models\": [\"grok-4.6\"]},\n        )\n        assert res.status_code == 200, res.text\n        body = res.json()\n        assert not body.get(\"failure_reason\"), body\n        jobs = def ai_baseline_run(\n    session: SessionDep,\n    usr_context: UserAccessDep,\n    form: AiBaselineRunForm,\n) -> AiBaselineJobResult:\n    from ..db.models.ai_agent_jobs import AiAgentJob\n    from ..utils.ai.config import (\n        no_providers_message,\n        resolve_llm_credentials,\n        unique_voter_models,\n    )\n    from ..utils.ai.identity import build_ai_participant_key\n\n    _require_ai_admin(usr_context)\n    project = _require_project(session, usr_context, form.project_id)\n    if not bool(getattr(project, \"include_ai_agents\", False)):\n        return AiBaselineJobResult(failure_reason=\"AI agents are not included for this project\")\n    customer = usr_context.sess_customer\n    if resolve_llm_credentials(customer) is None:\n        return AiBaselineJobResult(failure_reason=no_providers_message())\n    alts, factors = _load_content(session, project)\n    active_alts = [a for a in alts if not getattr(a, \"disabled\", False)]\n    if len(active_alts) < 2:\n        return AiBaselineJobResult(failure_reason=\"Save at least two options before running AI agents\")\n    requested = unique_voter_models(getattr(project, \"ai_voter_models\", None))\n    if not requested:\n        return AiBaselineJobResult(failure_reason=\"Select at least one AI agent in project settings\")\n    existing_parts = ProjectVoteParticipant.list_for_project_system(\n        session=session, customer_id=project.customer_id, project_id=project.id, clear_lock=False\n    )\n    parts_by_key = _ai_parts_by_key(existing_parts)\n    views: list[AiBaselineJobView] = []\n    latest = None\n    all_complete = True\n    for model_key in requested:\n        creds = resolve_llm_credentials(customer, model_key=model_key)\n        if creds is None:\n            return AiBaselineJobResult(failure_reason=no_providers_message())\n        part = parts_by_key.get(build_ai_participant_key(creds.model_key))\n        if part is not None and part.is_complete:\n            views.append(_job_view(None, participant_complete=True, display_name=creds.display_name))\n            continue\n        existing_job = AiAgentJob.get_active_for_project(\n            session=session,\n            customer_id=int(project.customer_id),\n            project_id=int(project.id),\n            model_key=creds.model_key,\n            clear_lock=False,\n        )\n        if existing_job is not None:\n            all_complete = False\n            latest = existing_job\n            views.append(_job_view(existing_job, participant_complete=False, display_name=creds.display_name))\n            continue\n        all_complete = False\n        enqueued = AiAgentJob.enqueue_baseline(\n            session=session,\n            customer_id=int(project.customer_id),\n            project_id=int(project.id),\n            requested_by_user_id=int(usr_context.sess_user.id),\n            model_key=creds.model_key,\n            details={\"provider\": creds.provider, \"source\": creds.source, \"display_name\": creds.display_name},\n            clear_lock=False,\n        )\n        latest = enqueued.job_info\n        views.append(_job_view(enqueued.job_info, participant_complete=False, display_name=creds.display_name))\n    return AiBaselineJobResult(\n        job=_job_view(latest, participant_complete=all_complete) if latest else (views[0] if views else
